### 154. ✅ Banner Carousel — Slide ကူးပြောင်းစဉ် Page Jump/Lurch ပြင်ဆင်
- **What changed:** Home (`welcome.blade.php`) + Glass Finder banner ရဲ့ text slides တွေကို `x-show` (display:none) ကနေ **CSS Grid stacking** (`col-start-1 row-start-1`) + opacity transition ပုံစံပြောင်းခဲ့ — slide အားလုံးက တစ်တန်းတည်းမှာ stack ဖြစ်နေလို့ container height က အမြင့်ဆုံး slide အတိုင်း တည်ငြိမ်နေပြီး fade လုပ်တဲ့အခါ page မလှုပ်တော့ပါ။ Hidden slide တွေကို `pointer-events-none` + `aria-hidden` ထားလို့ click / accessibility မထိခိုက်ပါ။ Leave transition ကနေ translate-y ဖယ်ပြီး pure fade ဖြစ်အောင်လုပ်ခဲ့။ "Special Offer" ကို translation key (`messages.special_offer`) အဖြစ် **en / my / zh_CN** သုံးဘာသာလုံး တစ်ပြိုင်တည်းထည့်ခဲ့။
- **Files:** `resources/views/welcome.blade.php`, `resources/views/storefront/glass_finder/index.blade.php`, `lang/en/messages.php`, `lang/my/messages.php`, `lang/zh_CN/messages.php`
- **Run:** `php artisan test` → **380 passed / 1910 assertions / 0 failures** (12.19s); `npm run build` → ✅
- **Hosting မှာ:** `npm run build` လို (grid stacking classes: `col-start-1` / `row-start-1`)

### 158. ✅ Deploy Script — Hostinger Split-Layout Front Controller Fix
- **What changed:** `deploy-datapos.sh` က webroot (`public_html/`) မှာ local standard `public/index.php` ကို upload လုပ်လို့ split layout (`public_html` + `laravel_app`) နဲ့ မကိုက်တဲ့အတွက် live `datapos.com` မှာ **500** ဖြစ်ခဲ့တယ်။ Script ရဲ့ post-deploy block မှာ split-layout `public_html/index.php` ကို write ပြီး `../laravel_app/vendor/autoload.php` + `../laravel_app/bootstrap/app.php` သို့ ညွှန်းအောင် fix လုပ်ထားပါတယ် — remote `.env`, vendor, storage တို့ကို overwrite မလုပ်ပါ။ Live site ကို HTTP 200 OK ထိပြန်စစ်ထားပြီး `php artisan optimize:clear` + `config:cache` + `view:cache` ပြီးပါပြီ။
- **Files:** `deploy-datapos.sh`
- **Validate:** `ssh` → `cat index.php` + `curl https://datapos.com` → **HTTP 200 OK**
- **Next:** Future deploy မှာ `deploy-datapos.sh` ပြန်သုံးရင် index.php က split layout အတိုင်း အလိုအလျောက် overwrite ဖြစ်လို့ နောက်တစ်ခါ 500 မဖြစ်ရပါ။

### 163. ✅ Floating Cart Widget — Draggable + Theme-Matched Color
- **What changed:**
  - **Draggable:** Order Builder floating widget ကို ဖုန်းမှာ လက်နဲ့ဖိပြီး ရွေ့လို့ရအောင် touch/mouse drag-and-drop လုပ်ထား (Alpine `x-data` ထဲမှာ touchstart/touchmove/touchend + mousedown/mousemove/mouseup handlers)။ 6px ထက်ပိုရွေ့မှ drag အဖြစ်ယူဆပြီး tap/click က ဆက်လုပ်နိုင်တယ်။ ရွေ့ပြီးနေရာကို `localStorage('cartWidgetPos')` မှာ သိမ်းပြီး reload ရင် နေရာ ပြန်ရတယ်။ Screen edge clamp (8px margin) + top/bottom safe zone (60px–80px) ပါ။
  - **Theme-matched color:** အဟောင်းက `bg-slate-900/85` (ချောင်းကြီးတွင်း dark) ဖြစ်တာကို light theme မှာ `bg-white/90` + dark theme မှာ `dark:bg-slate-900/85` ဖြစ်အောင် ပြင်။ Text color လည်း light မှာ `text-slate-900` + `text-sky-600` / dark မှာ `dark:text-white` + `dark:text-sky-400` အဖြစ် ပြင်။ Badge border ကလည်း `border-white dark:border-slate-900` ဖြစ်။ Shadow က `shadow-violet-500/40` → `shadow-sky-500/30` အဖြစ် နည်းနည်းသျော့။
- **Files:** `resources/views/layouts/storefront/app.blade.php`
- **Hosting:** `npm run build` မလိုပါ — existing Tailwind utilities ပဲ။

### 185. ✅ Admin Sidebar Fixes — Collapsed Labels, Single-Open Accordion, Focus Management
- **What changed:** Sidebar inspect/fix pass —
  - **Root cause found:** “ပင်မ Banner များ” (Home Banners) direct link label က sidebar ထဲက **တစ်ခုတည်းသော** `:class="sidebarCollapsed ? 'lg:hidden' : ''"` binding မပါတဲ့ label — collapsed 80px rail မှာ `truncate` ညှပ်ပြီး Burmese အပိုင်းအစ ကျန်နေတာ; binding ထည့် → collapsed မှာ `display:none` (clipping မဟုတ်ဘဲ လုံးဝပျောက်)
  - **Icon centering:** Dashboard + Home Banners + group buttons ၆ ခုလုံး → `:class="sidebarCollapsed ? 'lg:justify-center' : ''"`; accessible names (`aria-label`) + collapsed-only desktop tooltips (`:title`)
  - **Single-open accordion:** `toggleGroup(name)` — တစ်ခုဖွင့်ရင် ကျန်အကုန်ပိတ် · current page group auto-open (route-aware) · open group ပြန်နှိပ်ရင် ပိတ် · collapse လုပ်ရင် အကုန်ပိတ် · collapsed icon နှိပ်ရင် sidebar ကျယ် + group ပွင့် (real template click နဲ့ verify)
  - **Corner badge:** Sales group အတွက် collapsed-desktop corner badge (`:class="sidebarCollapsed && viewportLg ? 'inline-flex' : 'hidden'"`) — icon ကို မဖုံး, mobile မှာ မပေါ်
  - **Drawer focus:** open → focus က Close menu ထဲဝင် (cross-scope `$refs` ပြဿနာကို scoped query နဲ့ပြင်) · Escape/backdrop/close → focus က hamburger ကိုပြန်ရောက် · drawer closed မှာ `:inert`
- **Files:** `resources/views/layouts/admin/app.blade.php`, `tests/Feature/AdminSidebarNavigationUXTest.php`

### 188. ✅ Brands CRUD Safety — Safe Delete (Blocked When In Use) + Unique Name Constraint
- **What changed:** Brand management မှာ အန္တရာယ်ရှိတဲ့ နှစ်ချက်ကို ကာကွယ် —
  - **Safe destroy:** `brands` table က `brand_id -> nullable -> nullOnDelete()` ဖြစ်လို့ products ရှိတဲ့ Brand ကို ဖျက်ရင် အဲဒီ products တွေရဲ့ `brand_id` ကို **တိတ်တဆိတ် null ဖြစ်စေတယ်** → `destroy()` မှာ `products()->count() > 0` ဆို back + validation-style errors (ဘယ်တော့မှ null မလုပ်); UI မှာ Delete button ကို block-state နဲ့ အစားထိုး — "Used by N products" + products filter link (`?brand_id=`) — ဖျက်လို့ရတဲ့ brand (0 products) ကသာ confirmation modal နဲ့ ဖျက်လို့ရ
  - **Unique (store_id, name):** migration `2026_08_08_000001_add_unique_name_to_brands_table.php` — audit ပြီးမှထည့် (duplicate 0 / slug 0 — 61 brands); controller မှာ case-insensitive trimmed normalized check (`LOWER(TRIM(name))`) + ignore-self on update; whitespace-only name ကို reject; MySQL collation က controller နဲ့ကိုက်
  - **per_page safety:** `all`→100000 mapping ဖျက် — `ALLOWED_PER_PAGE` whitelist (25/50/100) ပဲ; ကျန်တာတွေ fallback 25 (unbounded query မဖြစ်အောင်)
  - **highlight_brand flash:** create ပြီးရင် list ပြန်ရောက်တဲ့အခါ အသစ်ဖန်တီးထားတဲ့ brand ကို scroll + highlight (pink ring + NEW badge)
- **Files:** `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/BrandController.php`, `database/migrations/2026_08_08_000001_add_unique_name_to_brands_table.php` (new), `lang/{my,en,zh_CN}/messages.php` (blocked/taken/required/used_by/view_products keys)
- **Verified:** duplicate audit 0 collisions · migration `[25] Ran` on local dev DB · destroy block + unique validation covered in AdminBrandTest (35 tests)

### 189. ✅ Brands UI/UX — Debounced Live Search + Reusable Logo Uploader + Tabbed Add/Edit + Accessible Delete Modal
- **What changed:** Brands list/add/edit ကို clean design system နဲ့အညီ ပြန်ရေး —
  - **Live search:** toolbar မှာ opt-in `liveSearch` prop — 450ms Alpine `@input.debounce` + spinner + clear control; query params (sort/filter/per_page) အကုန် preserve; mobile overlay search ပါ အလုပ်လုပ်
  - **Logo uploader (reusable `x-admin.logo-uploader`):** instant preview (object-URL — revoke) · filename/size · remove-selected · client-side type/size warning (server validation က authoritative) · keep/replace/remove states (နှစ်ခု တစ်ပြိုင်နက် မဖြစ်နိုင် — replace ရွေးရင် remove ပျက်) · PNG/JPG/WebP + square 1:1 recommendation · Add + Edit နှစ်နေရာလုံးမှာ သုံး
  - **Add tab:** autofocus name · list toolbar (search/filter/sort/per-page) ကို Add tab မှာ မပြ · field-level errors · validation fail ရင် Add tab ကို auto-return · "Saving…" disabled state (double-submit prevention) · success → list + highlight
  - **Edit page:** `max-w-2xl` responsive form · current logo + keep/replace/remove ရှင်းရှင်းခွဲ · dirty beforeunload warn · consistent pink primary
  - **Delete modal (accessible):** `window.confirm()` အစား — role=dialog + aria-modal · Cancel initial focus · Escape/backdrop close · focus trap (`trapFocus` — `getAttribute('tabindex') !== '-1'` filter) · close ရင် triggering button ကို focus ပြန် · "Deleting…" disabled state · data-id/name ကို trusted rendered data ကပဲယူ (stale row မဖြစ်)
  - **Responsive:** desktop clean table · mobile stacked cards (no page-level overflow, 44px touch targets) · long names/slugs break-words/truncate · dark mode
- **Files:** `resources/views/components/admin/logo-uploader.blade.php` (new), `resources/views/components/admin/toolbar.blade.php` (liveSearch), `resources/views/admin/brands/index.blade.php`, `resources/views/admin/brands/edit.blade.php`, `lang/{my,en,zh_CN}/messages.php`
- **Verified:** live browser — create→highlight ✓ · live search filter ✓ · duplicate rejection (error flash + Add tab return + field error) ✓ · delete modal (Cancel focus → Escape close → focus return → real delete → success flash, 62→61) ✓ · blocked state links ✓ · uploader preview blob ✓ · mobile 397px no overflow ✓ · 0 console errors

### 190. ✅ Brands Tests (AdminBrandTest 35) + FlashSale Regression Fix + Runtime Bug Fixes
- **What changed:**
  - **`tests/Feature/AdminBrandTest.php` (new, 35 tests):** list access · authorized staff · unauthorized rejected · cross-store access/edit/delete rejected · create with/without logo · logo optimized/stored (WebP) · invalid type rejected · oversized rejected · empty/whitespace-only name rejected · duplicate normalized name (same store) rejected · same name different store allowed · rename · logo replace · logo remove · unused brand deletable · **used brand not deletable** (associations + logo file preserved) · search by name/slug · logo filter · sort modes · pagination + safe per-page · query persistence · products filter link — production-ID-independent
  - **FlashSaleHomeSectionTest regression fix:** unique constraint အသစ်ကြောင့် same-store "Deal Brand" ×4 ထပ်ခဲ့ → brand name ကို `Str::random` suffix ထည့် (slug က ရှိပြီးသား unique) — 3/3 pass ပြန်
  - **Runtime bug fixes (visual verification နဲ့ဖမ်းမိ):** (a) `x-data` attribute ထဲ `[tabindex="-1"]` — HTML attribute မှာ backslash-escape မရှိလို့ attribute က ပထမ `"` မှာ ပြတ်ပြီး Alpine source ~200 chars က visible text အဖြစ် leak + Alpine scope လုံးဝ broken → `getAttribute('tabindex') !== '-1'` JS filter နဲ့ပြင်; (b) delete modal က `x-data` scope အပြင်မှာ ရှိနေလို့ `confirmTarget` resolve မဖြစ် → modal ကို scope အတွင်းပြောင်း; (c) desktop/mobile row နှစ်ခုလုံး `id="brand-row-{id}"` ထပ်နေလို့ mobile highlight scroll က hidden desktop row ကို ပစ်မှတ်ထား → mobile id `brand-row-m-{id}` + visible row ကို scroll; (d) `this.$refs.lastDelete = el` → plain `lastDeleteEl` data property (unidiomatic refs assignment ရှောင်)
- **Files:** `tests/Feature/AdminBrandTest.php` (new), `tests/Feature/FlashSaleHomeSectionTest.php`, `resources/views/admin/brands/index.blade.php`, `resources/views/components/admin/logo-uploader.blade.php`
- **Verified:** brand suite 35/35 (118 assertions) · brand+admin+localization batch 77/77 (454) · full suite 406 pass / 8 fail (အကုန် pre-existing — login branding/migration/order request/quick-login, stash-verified) · `npm run build` ✓ · `view:cache` ✓ · live browser end-to-end ✓

### 198. ✅ Admin List Filters Preserved Across Edit Round-Trips — `AdminListReturn` Helper
- **Problem:** filtered admin list (search/filter/sort/per_page) ကနေ Edit → update လုပ်ပြီးတဲ့အခါ **filters တွေ အကုန်ပျောက်ပြီး plain list ကို ပြန်ကျတယ်** — `update()`/`store()` redirects တွေက `/store/.../admin/...` ကို query string မပါပဲ ပြန်လွှဲနေလို့
- **Root cause:** redirect ၅ နေရာ (Brands, Categories, Products ×2, GlassFinder) + Banners/Users တွေမှာပါ အတူတူပဲ — query string drop
- **Fix:** shared helper `app/Support/AdminListReturn.php` (new):
  - `capture()` — index render လုပ်တုန်း current list URI (**relative path+query** — host-agnostic) session ထဲသိမ်း
  - `peek()` — edit/create ရဲ့ back links အတွက် ဖတ်ရုံပဲ (**session ကို မဖျက်**)
  - `resolve()` — update/store redirect အတွက် ဖတ်ပြီး consume (stale reuse မဖြစ်အောင်) · `/store/` prefix + scheme မပါတဲ့ URI ပဲ လက်ခံ (open-redirect safe)
- **Wired into 6 controllers:** BrandController, CategoryController, ProductController (store+update, create+edit), GlassFinderAdminController, UserManagementController, HomeBannerController — edit/create → `peek()`, update/store → `resolve()`; edit views ၇ ခုရဲ့ back links → `$returnTo ?? fallback`
- **Files:** `app/Support/AdminListReturn.php` (new), 6 admin controllers, 7 edit views (+`products/create`), `tests/Feature/AdminFilterReturnRoundTripTest.php` (new), `database/factories/UserFactory.php` (+`phone` — users.phone NOT NULL)
- **Verified:** round-trip feature test 2/2 (list capture → edit **မဖျက်** → update redirect `?search=filtered` ပြန်ထိန်း ✓ + no-prior-visit fallback ✓) · 105 tests pass · build ✓

### 199. 🐛 Peek/Resolve Consume Bug — Image Update လုပ်ပြီးရင် Filters ပြောင်းသွားတာ
- **Problem (user report):** ပုံ update လုပ်လိုက်ရင် ရွေးထားတဲ့ filters တွေ ပြောင်းသွားတယ်
- **Root cause:** item 198 ရဲ့ ပထမဗားရှင်းမှာ `resolve()` က session key ကို **forget** လုပ်တယ် — `edit()` (GET) မှာ back link အတွက် resolve ခေါ်လိုက်တာနဲ့ key ပျက်ပြီး နောက် update POST မှာ `resolve()` က fallback ပြန်ကျ → filter ပျောက်
- **Fix:** `peek()` ထပ်ထည့် — edit/create က peek (မဖျက်), update/store ကပဲ resolve (ဖျက်) · call sites ၇ ခု ပြောင်း
- **Verified:** `AdminFilterReturnRoundTripTest` — “edit page does not consume the filtered list session key” ✓ + browser — categories `?search=accessor` → edit → back link preserved ✓ · 108 tests pass · build ✓

### 202. ✅ Separate Brand Assets — Storefront / Admin / Favicon Logos (legacy fallback preserved)
- **Goal:** one `logo_path` ကို storefront header + admin sidebar + login + invoice + OG + favicon အကုန်သုံးနေလို့ horizontal logo က admin sidebar square ထဲမှာ မဖတ်ရလွယ် → **3 independent assets** + backward-compatible fallback
- **Migration:** `2026_08_09_000001_add_brand_asset_paths_to_storefront_settings_table` — `storefront_logo_path`, `admin_logo_path`, `favicon_path` (nullable, **`logo_path` ကို မဖျက်/မပြောင်း**) — production data safe
- **Model (`StorefrontSetting`):** fillable + resolution helpers — `storefrontLogo()` = `storefront_logo_path ?: logo_path` · `adminLogo()` = `admin_logo_path ?: storefront_logo_path ?: logo_path` · `favicon()` = `favicon_path ?: admin_logo_path ?: storefront_logo_path ?: logo_path` (static `favicon.ico` က DB asset လုံးဝမရှိမှ နောက်ဆုံးကျ)
- **Controller (`StoreSettingController::update`):** 3 file fields + per-field remove flags, **safe sequencing** — validate → store new → save DB → old file ကို save success ပြီးမှ ဖျက် · DB fail ဆို new orphans ဖျက် + old paths/files ထိန်း · တခြား column ကဆက်သုံးနေတဲ့ path ကို ဘယ်တော့မှ မဖျက် (legacy `logo_path` fallback အနေနဲ့လည်း ကာကွယ်)
- **UI:** General Settings မှာ uploader card **၃ ခု** — Storefront Logo (3:1, wide preview LIGHT+DARK), Admin Logo (square, 48×48 + 32×32 chips), Favicon (512×512, 32×32 + 16×16 chips) · reusable `components/admin/settings/sections/brand-asset.blade.php` + `Alpine.data('brandAssetUploader')` (isolated state per card: selectedFile/previewUrl/fileError/isValidFile/isSubmitting/markRemove, object-URL revoke, remove↔select mutual exclusion, `aria-live="polite"`, `role="alert"`, double-submit guard)
- **Consumers updated (8):** storefront header + favicon/OG (`($setting ?? null)?->method()` — undefined `$setting` warning-safe) · admin sidebar (square `object-contain`, 40–48 expanded / 32–40 collapsed) + admin head favicon (self-contained store resolution) · auth login/register + invoice → storefront fallback · `OptimizeImages` command → 3 new directories
- **Files:** migration (new), `StorefrontSetting.php`, `StoreSettingController.php`, `app.js`, `brand-asset.blade.php` (new), general/edit settings views, storefront/admin layouts, auth ×2, invoice, `AdminBrandAssetsTest.php` (new, 25 tests)
- **Verified:** 25 tests pass (fallback chain, upload, replace-deletes-only-replaced, remove-deletes-file + fallback re-engages, invalid/oversized rejection, transparency preserved, DB-failure orphan cleanup, accessible labels) · full suite **486 passed (2338 assertions)** · build ✓

### 203. 🐛 `Alpine.data` Registered After `Alpine.start()` — Uploader Cards Broken in Browser
- **Problem (browser QA):** settings page console မှာ `ReferenceError: brandAssetUploader is not defined` + state props ၁၃ ခုလုံး undefined — uploader card ၃ ခုလုံး inert
- **Root cause:** `Alpine.data('brandAssetUploader', …)` ကို `resources/js/app.js` ထဲ **`Alpine.start()` (line 469) ပြီးမှ** (line 610) register လုပ်ထား — Alpine v3 က start မတိုင်ခင်မှာပဲ registered components တွေကိုပဲ `x-data` scope အတွက် ရရှိနိုင်
- **Fix:** block တစ်ခုလုံးကို `Alpine.start()` ရှေ့ကို ရွှေ့ (line 473) — rebuild ပြီးနောက် console သန့် + card ၃ ခုလုံး state transitions (file select → preview / mark remove → fallback / cancel) အလုပ်လုပ်တာ browser မှာ အတည်ပြု
- **Files:** `resources/js/app.js`

### 209. ✅ Cloud Hue CSS Variable Pattern — `.cloud-hue-bg` + `--cloud-hue` (commit c0dda9d)
- **What changed:** `favorites.blade.php` brand cloud tiles inline gradient (server PHP `hsl(...)` L55 + Alpine string-concat `:style` L131) → **CSS custom property pattern** — `app.css` မှာ `.cloud-hue-bg` class (135deg gradient, `hsl(var(--cloud-hue, 210)…)`, hue angle ဖြစ်လို့ 360+ ကို CSS က သူ့အလိုလို wrap) · markup က `class="cloud-hue-bg"` + `style="--cloud-hue: {{ $cloudHue }}"` (server) / `:style="'--cloud-hue: ' + h"` (Alpine) · gradient logic CSS ထဲ တစ်နေရထဲ ပေါင်း — server/client shade 1% ကွာနေတာ ပြန်ညှိ · static inline `style="` count 7→4 (ကျန် ၄ ခုလုံး justified: richtext dynamic, favorites `--cloud-hue` var, catalog ×2 max+env) · scan: non-admin inline gradients **0 ကျန်** (browse pills / glass-finder chips က Tailwind gradient utilities — hue-based မဟုတ်၊ var pattern မလို)
- **Why:** inline gradient duplication ×2 sources → single source of truth; server + Alpine cards က hue/gradient byte-identical ဖြစ်အောင်
- **Files:** `resources/css/app.css` (`.cloud-hue-bg`), `resources/views/customer/account/favorites.blade.php` (L55 + L131)

- **What changed:** Settings ကို structured control center အဖြစ် အဆင့်မြှင့် — hard-coded footer payment badges (KPay/WavePay/CB Pay/MMQR/COD SVG) ဖျက်၊ admin-managed methods တွေနဲ့ အစားထိုး

- **Models:** `StorePaymentMethod` (maskedAccountNumber/maskedAccountName — Admin summary မှာ digits masked) · `StoreDeliveryMethod` · `Store` → paymentMethods()/deliveryMethods() hasMany · `StorefrontSetting` → mapUrl() (exact URL ?: address-search fallback), mapDirectionsUrl(), mapEmbedSrc() (coords ရှိမှသာ iframe — broken iframe ဘယ်တော့မှ မပြ)
- **Controller** `StoreSettingController`: contact section မှာ map validation (https-only scheme + Google-Maps-shaped URL check — `javascript:`/`data:` reject; shortened `maps.app.goo.gl` accept) + map fields normalize · **payment/delivery method CRUD** (store-scoped `$store->paymentMethods()->findOrFail()` — cross-store = 404 · `route('method')` — StoreContext DI နဲ့ positional binding မတိုက်ဖို့) · icon lifecycle (replace → save ပြီးမှ old ဖျက် · remove · delete မှာ icon file clean) · `hasMapInput` guard — partial contact save က map data မဖျက်
- **Admin UI:** contact မှာ Exact Store Location card (URL/lat/lng/title + enable + embed toggle + live map preview) · delivery section → structured method cards (Add inline form, edit/delete per row, built-in/custom/initials icon, active + show-account-details toggles) + Legacy Notes standalone form · **nested `<form>` bug fix** — delivery section ကို main settings form ထဲ မထည့်တော့ (HTML က nested forms မထောက် — CRUD buttons ပျက်စီးမယ်)
- **Storefront:** footer → structured payment icon chips (structured methods မရှိရင် legacy `payment_info` fallback) + concise delivery summary · How-to-Order + Order Builder → structured method cards (account details `show_account_details` ဖွင့်မှသာ ပြ) · map link → exact pin URL + Get Directions
- **Localization:** `get_directions` / `open_in_google_maps` / `visit_our_store` keys en/my/zh_CN · `how_to_hero_title` (missing key — raw `messages.how_to_hero_title` text ပေါ်နေတာ) · intro ထဲက embedded numbered-step lines တွေကို render မှာ strip (intro 6 steps vs 5 cards contradiction ပြေလည်)

### 227. ✅ Production CSP — Hostinger vhost override fixed
- **ဖြစ်စဉ်:** Deploy #13 ပြီးနောက် ပထမဆုံး production CSP verification မှာ — **Hostinger/LiteSpeed က vhost အဆင့်မှာ bare `Content-Security-Policy: upgrade-insecure-requests` inject လုပ်ပြီး middleware ရဲ့ nonce policy ကို ဖျောက်လိုက်တာ** (origin ကို SSH ကနေ တိုက်ရိုက် curl ပြီး အတည်ပြု; acdcmm.com ကတော့ full policy ပြနေတာ အတည်ပြု)
- **အဖြေ:** acdcmm-proven pattern — `public/.htaccess` ထဲမှာ directory-level `Header always set Content-Security-Policy "..."` (vhost header ပြီးမှ apply လို့ အနိုင်ရ) · **trade-off:** static header မှာ per-request nonce မထည့်လို့ production `script-src` = `'unsafe-inline' 'unsafe-eval'` (acdcmm နဲ့တူ) · nonce middleware က local မှာ ဆက်သုံး, vhost override ဖျက်ရင် production မှာပါ အလိုအလျောက် ဝင် · `docs/production-deployment-guide.md` မှာ quirk + verify command မှတ် — **`public/.htaccess` နဲ့ middleware ကို sync ထားရ**
- **Verify:** `curl -sI https://datapos.com/ | grep -i content-security-policy` → full policy (frame-src Google Maps + YouTube ပါ) ✓ · home/product/how-to-order 200 ✓

### 229. ✅ Backfill (session 1) — CSP Hardening with Nonces + Google Maps iframes
- *(အစောပိုင်း session မှာ ပြီးခဲ့တဲ့ အလုပ်တွေ — changelog မှာ နောက်ကြောင်းပြန် မှတ်တမ်းတင်ခြင်း)*
- **Nonce-based CSP:** `SecurityHeaders` middleware — per-request `base64_encode(random_bytes(18))` nonce → `View::share('cspNonce')` → Blade inline `<script>` တိုင်းမှာ `nonce="{{ $cspNonce }}"` · inline event handlers (onclick/onchange/…) တွေကို delegated listeners (`resources/js/csp-helpers.js`: `data-ios-href`, `data-catalog-view`, `data-auto-submit`, `data-confirm`, `data-print`, `data-img-fallback`) အဖြစ် ပြောင်း · `script-src` မှာ `'unsafe-inline'` ဖြုတ် (`'unsafe-eval'` က Alpine standard build အတွက် ဆက်ထား) · admin + auth + storefront layouts အကုန် · `app.js`/`app-admin.js` — CSP nonce API
- **Google Maps iframes:** `frame-src` မှာ `https://www.google.com https://maps.google.com https://www.youtube.com` ထည့် — footer map embed + how-to-order YouTube embeds အလုပ်လုပ်တယ်
- **Test:** 70 targeted tests + full suite green (511) ✓ · browser-verified (homepage console clean, nonce works, maps render, Viber iOS href swap) ✓ · `headers.txt` sample snapshot + `docs/production-deployment-guide.md` update ✓

### 232. ⚠️ CRITICAL FIX — Admin Product Edit: untouched save was clearing Brand (+ category/warranty safety)
- **Bug (data loss risk):** Product Edit page မှာ Brand dropdown က **preselect မဖြစ်ဘဲ blank option ပြ** — Alpine `x-model` က select ကို `x-for` options တွေ render မဖြစ်ခင် init လုပ်လို့ persisted value ကို မထည့်နိုင်တာ။ Form ကို Brand မပြောင်းဘဲ save လုပ်ရင် `brand_id=""` submit ဖြစ်ပြီး existing brand **ဖျက်ခံရ**။ Storefront + preview က brand ကို state ကနေ ပြနေလို့ "preview မှာ Xiaomi ပြ / dropdown မှာ blank" ဆိုတဲ့ ကွဲလွဲချက်နဲ့ ရှာတွေ့တယ်
- **Root cause:** Brand select မှာ `x-init="$nextTick(() => $el.value = selectedBrand)"` **မပါဘူး** — main/sub-category selects တွေမှာ ရှိပြီးသား race fix ကို brand မှာ ထည့်ဖို့ မေ့ထားတာ (audit နဲ့ အတည်ပြု: brand တစ်ခုတည်းပဲ x-for options + no x-init; variant preset selects ၂ ခုကလည်း ဒီပုံစံပဲ ဒါပေမယ့် transient UI state ဖြစ်လို့ data-loss မဖြစ်)
- **Fix 1 (form):** Brand select ကို category နဲ့ တူညီတဲ့ `x-init="$nextTick(() => $el.value = selectedBrand)"` ထည့် — options render ပြီးမှ persisted value ကို ပြန်ထည့် → untouched form က brand_id=60 ကို မှန်မှန် submit လုပ်တယ်
- **Fix 2 (controller hardening, defense-in-depth):** `update()` မှာ nullable relationship fields — `brand_id`, `category_id`, `warranty`, `return_policy` — ကို `$request->has(...) ? ($validated[...] ?? null) : $product->...` ပြောင်း — **request ထဲ field မပါရင် existing value preserve** · `''` ပို့ရင်တော့ intentional clear (validation `nullable` အတိုင်း) · Laravel 12 `has()` က presence-based (empty string ပါ present လို့ ရေတွက်) လို့ absent vs blank ကို ခွဲနိုင်တယ်
- **သက်ရောက်မှု:** production product 1538 (RM-N10PRO(5G)-BD-BLK, brand_id=60 Xiaomi) **မပျက်စီးသေး** — user က testing မှာ ဖမ်းမိလို့; DB စစ်ပြီး အတည်ပြု (brand 60, category 50, warranty intact)
- **Test:** `ProductEditBrandPreservationTest` အသစ် 7 tests / 17 assertions — edit page preselect (state + x-init + preview) · untouched save preserve · **field absent → preserve** · change brand → persist · explicit blank → clear · category absent → preserve · warranty absent→preserve / blank→clear · full suite **539 passed / 2611 assertions** ✓
- **Live verify (dev):** 1538 edit page → select shows Xiaomi (state 60 = DOM 60 = preview Xiaomi) · real FormData submit untouched → DB brand 60 / category 50 / warranty intact ✅ · **Deploy #16** → production product page 200 + Xiaomi visible + admin route auth ✅

### 233. ✅ Product Detail — Return Policy Disclosure + Product SEO Meta (canonical/OG/Twitter)
- **Return Policy (storefront):** Warranty အောက်မှာ **collapsible disclosure** — Alpine button + `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls` + keyboard (native button) · **collapsed default** (ဖုန်းမှာ action buttons တွေကို မတွန်းချ) · no-JS ဆိုရင် panel open ဖြစ်နေလို့ text မပျောက် · `whitespace-pre-line` နဲ့ line breaks preserve · **`{{ }}` escaped plain text — raw HTML မဖြစ်** · whitespace-only policy → section လုံးဝမပြ (trim check)
- **Meta Description SEO:** central `App\Support\SeoMeta` helper — fallback chain: `meta_description` → plain-text `description` (tags strip + entities decode + whitespace normalize + **script/style block လုံးဝဖျက်**) → generic summary (`{name} ကို {store} တွင် ကြည့်ရှုဝယ်ယူနိုင်ပါသည်…`) → store default (layout) · **Unicode-safe word-boundary truncation ~160** (Burmese mb cut မပြတ်) · layout ကတစ်နေရာတည်းကနေ `meta description` + `og:description` + `twitter:description` output · **duplicate tags မရှိ**
- **Canonical/OG/Twitter:** product page က clean `$canonicalUrl` ပို့လို့ canonical + og:url က query-free product URL · `robots index,follow` · `og:type=product` · og:title=product name · **og:image** = product image → မရှိရင် **store share logo fallback** (pre-existing bug ပြင် — product page တွေမှာ $setting မရောက်လို့ layout fallback မီးမလောင်ဘူးဆိုတာ ရှာတွေ့) · meta description ကို body content အဖြစ် ဘယ်နေရာမှ မပြ
- **Admin UX:** return_policy → **textarea** (field name မပြောင်း) + storefront preview section မှာ live return policy preview (DOM-read pattern — x-data ထဲ string မထည့်) · meta description → helper text (exact task text) + **0/160 counter** (160 ကျော်ရင် အနီ warning — save block မလုပ်) + empty-fallback note
- **Controller hardening:** `update()` မှာ `description` + `meta_description` ကိုလည်း absent-field preserve (request မပါရင် existing မပျက်; `''` ပို့ရင်တော့ clear) — brand/category/warranty/return_policy နဲ့ အတူ
- **DB/schema/import column တွေ လုံးဝမပြောင်း** · lang keys 5 ခု × 3 locales
- **Test:** `ReturnPolicyAndSeoMetaTest` **16 tests / 56 assertions** — policy shown/hidden/escaped · edit form preserve · 3 SEO tags · description fallback (no tags) · generic fallback · rich-text no leak (script content ဖျက်) · **Burmese Unicode truncation** · quotes attribute-safe · no duplicate · canonical/robots/og:type · og:image product/absent/logo-fallback · unchanged save preserves all · full suite **555 passed / 2667 assertions** ✓

### 234. ✅ Viber Order Message — iOS swap မှာ product details ပျောက်နေတဲ့ bug ပြင်
- **ပြဿနာ:** Product Detail page ရဲ့ **Direct Order Viber button** — iPhone/iPad မှာ `data-ios-href` swap က `viber://contact?number=...` route ကို သုံးလို့ **contact route က draft (message) မသယ်နိုင်** → product name နဲ့ order details တွေ Viber chat ထဲ မရောက် (Android + Telegram ကတော့ `chat+draft` သုံးလို့ အဆင်ပြေနေတာ)
- **Fix:** `ContactLinkBuilder::viberIosContactUrl($phone, $draft = null)` — draft ပါရင် **chat route (`viber://chat?number=...&draft=...`) ကို ပြန်** (chat route က iOS မှာ + ဖြုတ်ထားလို့ အလုပ်လုပ် — encoded `+` က "Request Unavailable" ဖြစ်စေတဲ့ဟာ) · draft မပါတဲ့ generic contact links (footer/how-to-order) ကတော့ contact route ဆက်သုံး (private number reliability အတွက်) · product page က `$directOrderText` ကို draft အနေနဲ့ ထည့်ပို့
- **Audit:** Ask-about-stock, order confirmation ("Viber Send Order"), chat popup — အကုန် `chat+draft` တိုက်ရိုက်သုံးပြီးသား (ios swap မရှိ) → OK · footer/how-to-order က draft မလိုတဲ့ contact links → contact route ဆက်သုံး
- **Test:** `ContactLinkBuilderTest` 7 tests / 26 assertions — အသစ် 2: **iOS + draft → chat route နဲ့ message မပျောက်** · empty draft → contact route ဆက်သုံး · full suite **557 passed / 2670 assertions** ✓
- **Live verify:** dev — Direct Order button ရဲ့ href + data-ios-href နှစ်ခုလုံး draft ပါ (decoded: greeting, product name, SKU, price, link) ✓ · production Deploy #18 — `data-ios-href="viber://chat?number=959790444128&draft=..."` (product details အကုန်ပါ) ✓ · footer contact links က contact route အတိုင်း ✓

### 243. ✅ Invoice print stylesheet verified + dead Print button fixed

- Verified `@media print` rules: body→white/0 padding, `.sheet`→no shadow/radius 0/20px padding/max-width none, `.actions` (Print button)→hidden; rules confirmed in the live page's computed styles.
- **Bug found:** the invoice page is a standalone HTML doc with zero scripts, so the delegated `data-print` handler (csp-helpers.js) never loaded — the 🖨️ Print / Save PDF button did nothing.
- Fixed `resources/views/admin/orders/invoice.blade.php`: added a CSP nonce-stamped inline script (`nonce="{{ $cspNonce }}"`) wiring `[data-print]` → `window.print()`, matching the project's nonce-based CSP pattern (SecurityHeaders middleware, global).
- Verified live: script loads with nonce, click calls `window.print()`, no CSP violations in console; admin order suite 19 tests / 59 assertions green.

### 244. ✅ Invoice print pagination fixed (real PDF verification)

- Rendered the invoice to actual PDFs via headless Chrome print-to-pdf and reviewed pagination with pypdf (installed to a temp dir only).
- **Bug found:** the invoice had no `@page` rule, so Chrome printed US Letter with default margins and the break landed right after the items table — the normal invoice spilled **only "Total Due" + footer onto page 2** (orphaned totals).
- **Fix in `resources/views/admin/orders/invoice.blade.php`:** added `@page { size: A4; margin: 12mm; }`, `break-inside: avoid` on `.totals`/`.foot`, `thead { display: table-header-group }`, `tbody tr { break-inside: avoid }`, and wrapped table + totals + footer in a `.print-block { break-inside: avoid }` so the totals never separate from the table.
- **Verified:** normal invoice → **1 A4 page** (was 2); long-content invoice → 2 pages with a clean break before the table (header + customer info on page 1, full table + totals + footer together on page 2). Admin order suite 19 tests / 59 assertions green.

### 245. ✅ Admin Order Invoice — production-ready A4 print layout (full audit + rebuild)

**Root causes found:**
1. **Oversized Viber/Telegram icons** — `x-brand-icon` renders an SVG with only a `class` attribute (no width/height), and the standalone invoice page loads **no Tailwind**, so the `h-3.5 w-3.5` classes were inert and SVGs exploded to full width.
2. **Letter page + browser headers/footers** — no explicit `@page` A4 declaration; `window.print()` inherits Chrome's header/footer (date, title, URL, page numbers).
3. **Whitespace/wrapping** — loose spacing and no `overflow-wrap` for long order numbers, names, addresses, SKUs.
4. **Burmese rendering** — the page never loaded the app's Noto Sans Myanmar subset.

**Rebuilt `resources/views/admin/orders/invoice.blade.php`:**
- `@page { size: A4 portrait; margin: 10mm 12mm; }` + `@media print` isolation (`.no-print` hidden, `print-color-adjust: exact`).
- Compact two-column header (logo ≤150px + store name/subtitle; INVOICE + order number + date). Contact shown as **plain-text labels** (`Address/Phone/Viber/Telegram`) — no SVG icons → oversized-icon bug eliminated.
- Billed To / Status two-column grid (`minmax(0,1fr)`), compact status pills, boxed customer note; empty fields skipped; Viber/phone deduped.
- Fixed-layout items table (5/55/10/15/15), long SKU/variant wrap via `overflow-wrap:anywhere`, `thead` repeats per page, rows `break-inside:avoid`, numbers `nowrap`.
- Compact right-aligned totals (only existing values; Total Due emphasized), never orphaned.
- Burmese font stack + `@font-face` Noto Sans Myanmar resolved via `Vite::asset()`.
- Screen-only toolbar (Back + Print + Burmese print hint "Headers and footers") under `.no-print`.

**Verified by rendering real PDFs (headless Chrome, same-origin) + pypdf/PyMuPDF:**
- Normal 2-item → **1 A4 page**; long-text stress → **1 page**; 5-item → **1 page**; 20-item → **2 pages** with table header repeated on page 2 and totals on the final page.
- No blank pages, no horizontal overflow, no text past margins; Totals correct (Ks 146,000 / 25,000 / 1,652,000 / 70,000).
- Noto Sans Myanmar embedded in the PDF; browser headers/footers removed via `--no-pdf-header-footer` + print hint.
- Admin order suite 19 tests / 59 assertions green.

### 246. ✅ Home — Category cards redesigned to 1:1 photo tiles (photo on top, name below, borderless, no product count)

**Request:** Category cards in the "လူကြိုက်များသော အမျိုးအစားများ" strip → 1:1 photo on top (full width, borderless), category name below, product count removed.

**Changes:**
- `routes/web.php` (both home closures `/` and `/store/{store_slug}`) — each main category row now carries a `cover`: the newest featured (or latest) product's image in that category (product `image_path`, else its default variant's image), resolved in one query (`unique('category_id')` keeps the featured-most-recent product per category).
- `resources/views/welcome.blade.php` — card rebuilt: `w-36 sm:w-40` vertical card, `aspect-square` full-width photo container (rounded, no border), name centered below (`truncate`, sky hover), count line removed. Cover priority: category's own admin-uploaded `image_path` → representative product photo → emoji tile fallback (reuses the project's `data-img-fallback="hide-next"` pattern).
- `npm run build` — rebuilt assets so the new Tailwind utilities (`w-36`, `sm:w-40`, …) land in the compiled CSS.

**Verified live (preview):** 12 cards, square ratio 1.0, photo spans full card width, name below centered, no border/background card, no "N ပစ္စည်း" count; console clean, all requests 200.

**Note:** the local dev DB has no product/category images (0 rows in `products.image_path`, `product_variants.image_path`, `product_images`), so locally the emoji fallback tiles show — matching the rest of the local catalog. On production (which has product images) real photos render. Category owners can also upload a dedicated photo per category in Admin → Categories, which takes priority.

### 247. ✅ Deploy #25 — 1:1 category photo cards live + category photos restored (production)

**Request:** Deploy the new 1:1 category photo cards, then upload a real category photo and verify it on the live home page.

**Code deployed (from working tree):**
- `resources/views/welcome.blade.php` + `routes/web.php` — new 1:1 photo category cards (changelog 246).
- `deploy-datapos.sh` — **added `--exclude='./.freebuff'`**: the Freebuff desktop DB (`desktop-v2.db`, ~47 MB, gitignored) was being tar'd into every deploy and made uploads time out.

**Incident handled during deploy:** the SSH pipe to Hostinger kept dropping. Step [2/3] shipped Laravel's stock `public/index.php`, and the [3/3] rewrite to the split-layout version never ran → site went 500 (empty `../vendor/autoload.php` path) and one retry truncated `index.php` to 0 bytes (200-empty). Fixed by writing the correct split-layout `index.php` (535 bytes) via base64+`base64 -d` (the only transfer method that survived the flaky connection). Post-deploy composer/`package:discover`/caches completed manually.

**Category photos (production DB):** production had **zero images anywhere** (products/variants/gallery/categories all empty) — every card fell back to emoji. Found 5 real orphaned category photos in production storage (`storage/app/public/categories/`, uploaded Aug 8, references lost in the category re-import), identified them visually, and restored references on the correct main categories:
- **Electronic (90)** → microchip `9lFgDkSu…webp`
- **Phone Spare Parts (108)** → precision tools `CDIhRf3…webp`
- **Phone Accessories (88)** → dock/earbuds/smartwatch `QJVwkTy…jpg`
- **Power & Storage (85)** → sun `bMhpY4…webp`
- Reverted a mistaken earlier write that had put the sun image on sub-category **Microphone (119)** → back to NULL.
- (Gaming-controller photo `vrbgYQ…webp` left unassigned — no clean match.)

**Live verification (https://datapos.com/store/datapos-mobile):** HTTP 200, 12 new cards, and the 4 cards above render their photos (`storage/categories/…`) inside the 1:1 squares; the rest show emoji fallback. `index.php` restored, no leftover temp scripts on the remote, local temp files removed.

**Notes:** local dev DB is NOT synced with production (different category IDs — production uses 78–119, local 112–142), so these photo assignments exist only on production. `git` changes (routes/web.php, welcome.blade.php, deploy-datapos.sh, changelog) are still uncommitted — commit/push on request.

### 248. ✅ PWA support — manifest + service worker + install banner (deployed)

- **`public/manifest.webmanifest`** — name "DataPOS", short_name "DataPOS", start_url "/", display standalone, background #f1f5f9, theme #C8102E, orientation portrait-primary, icons 192/512 (generated from the existing 180px apple-touch-icon logo via GD).
- **`public/icons/icon-192.png`, `public/icons/icon-512.png`** — upscaled logo PNGs (white background, purpose any+maskable).
- **`public/sw.js`** — app-shell service worker (root scope `/`):
  - Navigations: stale-while-revalidate (serve cached, refresh in background, offline fallback to cached homepage).
  - Static assets (CSS/JS/fonts/images): cache-first + background refresh; miss → network.
  - **Never cached:** non-GET, cross-origin, `/api/`, `/products/suggestions`, `/glass-finder/favorite`, `order-builder` (cart), `/orders` (checkout), `/checkout`, `/account`, `/admin`, auth pages, `/reviews`, `/favorite`.
  - Install/`SKIP_WAITING` message bridge; `skipWaiting` + old-cache purge on activate; `CACHE_VERSION` constant to bump per deploy.
- **`resources/views/layouts/storefront/app.blade.php`** — PWA meta tags in head (theme-color, apple-mobile-web-app-capable, mobile-web-app-capable, manifest link, apple-touch-icon /icons/icon-192.png); nonce-stamped SW registration; custom Tailwind install banner (bottom sheet above mobile nav) shown after the **3rd visit** when `beforeinstallprompt` fires; "Not now"/✕ dismiss permanently via localStorage (`pwa_dismissed`); accepted install sets `pwa_installed`; hidden in standalone mode and when already installed.
- **`lang/{en,my,zh_CN}/messages.php`** — `pwa_install_title/desc/btn/notnow` strings.
- **`public/.htaccess`** — `AddType application/manifest+json .webmanifest` (Hostinger served it as text/plain, which some browsers reject for installability).
- **Verified:** local `http://127.0.0.1:8500` — all tags render, SW registered, banner hidden until 3 visits, console clean; production `https://datapos.com/manifest.webmanifest` → 200 `application/manifest+json`, `/sw.js`, both icons 200, homepage carries all PWA tags. Deploy via `deploy-datapos.sh` (DEPLOY_OK).

### 249. ✅ Web Push backend — laravel-notification-channels/webpush (subscribe/unsubscribe/test + new-order alerts)

- **Package:** `composer require laravel-notification-channels/webpush` (v11.0.0, minishlink/web-push under the hood).
- **Migration** `2026_08_10_000004_create_push_subscriptions_table.php` — `id`, `user_id` (nullable FK → users, nullOnDelete), `endpoint` varchar(500) **UNIQUE** (text+unique is impossible on MySQL without a prefix, so varchar keeps the unique index working on both MySQL and SQLite), `keys` JSON, timestamps. MySQL/CloudBase + local SQLite compatible; ran cleanly locally.
- **`app/Models/PushSubscription`** — extends the package model so `WebPushChannel` works unchanged; fillable `endpoint/keys/user_id`; accessors bridge the JSON `keys` → the package's `public_key`/`auth_token`/`content_encoding`; `storeFromRequest()` (upsert by endpoint) + `removeSubscription()`.
- **`config/webpush.php`** — published; `model` → `App\Models\PushSubscription`; VAPID from `VAPID_SUBJECT` / `VAPID_PUBLIC_KEY` / `VAPID_PRIVATE_KEY` env.
- **`routes/api.php`** (registered via `withRouting(api:)` in bootstrap/app.php, `web` middleware group so session auth + CSRF work) — `POST /api/push/subscribe`, `DELETE /api/push/unsubscribe`, `POST /api/push/test` (admin-only: platform owner or store admin).
- **`PushNotificationController`** — subscribe (validates endpoint + keys), unsubscribe, test (validates title/body/url, sends to all subscribers, logs the last 20 sends to cache, guarded so one broken subscription never 500s the broadcast), index (admin page).
- **Notifications** — `NewOrderNotification` (title "New order received!", body with product + total, icon/badge = `/icons/icon-192.png`, click-through URL = the store's admin order page) and `TestPushNotification`. **Synchronous** (not queued): this deployment has no queue worker (`QUEUE_CONNECTION=database`, supervisor deferred), so a queued job would never deliver; dispatch sites are try/catch-guarded so pushes can't break ordering.
- **Order model / controller** — `OrderController::store` dispatches `NewOrderNotification` to the store's active `store_manager`/`staff` users after creation, best-effort.
- **Admin page** `resources/views/admin/push/index.blade.php` at `GET /store/{slug}/admin/push` (named `store.admin.push.index`, matches the app's store-scoped admin convention) — subscriber count, VAPID status, Send Test / custom title+body+URL form, recent-notifications table; sidebar link added under Tools. Inline script carries the CSP nonce.
- **`vapid:generate` command** — `app/Console/Commands/GenerateVapidKeys.php`; generates a P-256 pair, stores `VAPID_SUBJECT/PUBLIC_KEY/PRIVATE_KEY` in `.env` (with `--show`/`--force`). Falls back to direct `openssl_pkey_new` + EC details when minishlink's web-token path fails (XAMPP OpenSSL lacks a default openssl.cnf — detected `extras/openssl/openssl.cnf`). Keys generated for local `.env`; placeholders added to `.env.example`.
- **Tests** `tests/Feature/PushNotificationTest.php` — 12 tests / 35 assertions: guest + authed subscribe, upsert-not-duplicate, key validation, unsubscribe, admin-only test endpoint, no-subscriber error, keys-JSON → channel-attribute mapping, remove by endpoint, NewOrderNotification message shape, and order-creation → admin notification via the real storefront route.
- **Verified:** full suite 600 passed / 1 failed — the single failure (`ProductDetailTabsAndSpecsTest > direct order links rebuild with selected variant`) **pre-exists** my changes (confirmed via git stash) and concerns the earlier Viber-modal work. Live preview: admin push page renders, subscribe/upsert/unsubscribe round-trip works over the real API, test endpoint returns 200 and logs the send; `push_subscriptions` table left clean after testing.
- **Deploy status:** NOT deployed — runs locally against SQLite. Production (MySQL via CloudBase) needs the migration + `php artisan vapid:generate` on the server; follow the existing `deploy-datapos.sh` workflow when you're ready.

### 250. ✅ Admin push notifications for new orders / status / payment + history page (queued, deduped, Burmese)

- **Goal:** store admins get Web Push on order events — new order, order status change, payment received — with a permanent audit log and an admin history page. All notification text in Burmese.
- **Notifications** (`app/Notifications/`): `NewOrderNotification` rewritten (title `🆕 အော်ဒါ #ORD-…`, body `ဝယ်သူ: …၊ စုစုပေါင်း: Ks …၊ ပစ္စည်းအရေအတွက်: …`, icon `/icons/icon-192.png`, **badge `/icons/badge-72.png`** — 72×72 generated from the logo, click → `/store/{slug}/admin/orders/{id}`); new `OrderStatusNotification` (📦 `အော်ဒါ #… သည် အတည်ပြုပြီး/ပို့ဆောင်ပြီး/… ဖြစ်ပါပြီ`) and `PaymentReceivedNotification` (💵). All implement `ShouldQueue` (queued via `Notification::send`, `tries=3`, `afterCommit`) so order creation is never slowed; `TestPushNotification` now loggable as type `system`.
- **Dispatch:** single helper `app/Support/AdminPushNotifier` — **rate-limited** per event via `Cache::add` (atomic → double-clicks/retries can never send twice), targets the store's active `store_manager`/`staff`, writes the audit-log row, and swallows failures (never breaks ordering/admin updates). Wired into `OrderController::store` (order-created.{id}), `OrderAdminController::updateStatus` (order-status.{id}.{status}), `updateFinances` (order-payment.{id}.paid).
- **Log + history:** new migration `push_notification_logs` (`type order|payment|status|system`, title, body, url, recipient_count, sent_at) + `PushNotificationLog` model; admin page `/store/{slug}/admin/push/history` (sidebar "Push History" link) — last 50, filter pills by type, time/type badge/title (links to the order)/body/recipients. Test/custom sends now also write a `system` log row.
- **Delivery:** queued jobs sit in the `jobs` table (QUEUE_CONNECTION=database, no persistent worker) — added a scheduler entry in `routes/console.php` (`queue:work --once --stop-when-empty` every minute, skipped on sync driver) so shared hosting can deliver with one cron line (`* * * * * php artisan schedule:run`).
- **Tests** `tests/Feature/PushNotificationTest.php` — 19 tests / 63 assertions: Burmese message shape + badge-72, order dispatch dedupe + log row, status-change notify, double-status single-notification, payment notify, unpaid no-notify, history page render + type filter, history requires admin. Full suite 608 passed / 1 failed — the single failure (`ProductDetailTabsAndSpecsTest` Viber-link assertion) **pre-exists** this task.
- **Live-verified** in the local preview: admin status change on order 4 → log row `[status] 📦 အော်ဒါ #ORD-6A75FE9E74730 သည် အတည်ပြုပြီး ဖြစ်ပါပြီ` (recipients 1) on the history page; a second identical status update added **no** duplicate (dedupe works); 1 queued job in `jobs`; sidebar + history page render.
- **Deploy status:** NOT deployed — runs locally against SQLite. Production needs this migration + the earlier `push_subscriptions` migration + `php artisan vapid:generate` + the scheduler cron, then deploy via `deploy-datapos.sh`.

### 254. ✅ Multi-store readiness Phase 1 — primary-store resolver fix (root home survives store #2)

- **Goal (multi-store-ready-plan.md Phase 1):** store ၂ ခု active ဖြစ်လာရင် root `/` home မပျက်အောင် resolver ကို ပြင်တာ။ နောက်ခံ: `ResolveStoreContext` fallback က active store တစ်ခုတည်းရှိမှသာ ရွေးတယ် — store #2 ဖွင့်တာနဲ့ null ပြန်ပြီး home empty state ဖြစ်တယ်။
- **Migration** `2026_08_11_000001_add_is_primary_to_stores_table.php` — `stores.is_primary` boolean (default false, after `is_active`). SQLite + MySQL compatible (add column).
- **Model** `app/Models/Store.php` — `is_primary` → `$fillable` + `$casts` (boolean).
- **Resolver** `app/Http/Middleware/ResolveStoreContext.php` — fallback priority: (1) logged-in user's active store membership → (2) **primary active store** (အသစ် — two-primary data drift ရှိရင် lowest id ကို deterministic ရွေး) → (3) active store တစ်ခုတည်း (legacy) → (4) null (clear empty state, crash မဟုတ်)။
- **Dashboard** `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/DashboardController.php` — fallback ကိုလည်း primary-first နဲ့ တသမတ်တည်း (`Store::first()` → primary active → `Store::first()`).
- **Tests** `tests/Feature/StoreContextResolverTest.php` — 6 tests / 20 assertions (plan §7.1): two-active + primary → primary on root · no-primary single-active legacy · no-primary two-active → clear empty state (200, နှစ် store ဒေတာ မပေါ်) · user membership wins over primary · platform owner without membership → primary · two-primary drift → deterministic lowest id.
- **Verified:** Full suite **614 passed / 2950 assertions** (baseline 608 + 6 အသစ်, regression 0). Live preview: temp 2nd active store ထည့်ပြီး root `/` 200 + primary store (ACDC Branding Store) ပဲ render, temp store ဒေတာ မပေါ် · `/store/{slug}` store-scoped route 200 · မရှိတဲ့ slug 404 · temp store ဖျက်ပြီး primary တစ်ခုတည်း ပြန်သေချာ။
- **Deploy လုပ်ရမယ့်အချိန်:** `php artisan migrate` + production မှာ primary store သတ်မှတ် (`App\Models\Store::where('slug','datapos-mobile')->update(['is_primary'=>true])`) — plan §8 အတိုင်း။ Admin Store Management UI က Phase 2 (နောက် session)။

### 255. ✅ Multi-store readiness Phase 2 — Admin Store Management UI (platform owner)

- **Goal (multi-store-ready-plan.md Phase 2):** `production:create-store` CLI ကို Admin UI နဲ့ ဖြည့်စွက် — Platform Owner က browser ကနေ store list / create / edit / deactivate / reactivate လုပ်နိုင်အောင်။ Store ကို hard delete မလုပ် (deactivate-only, `is_active=false`), primary-single rule (primary store ကို deactivate/ဖျက်လို့ မရ — ဦးစွာ အခြား store ကို primary ပြောင်းရမယ်), last-active guard (နောက်ဆုံး active store ကို deactivate မလုပ်ရ)။
- **Controller** `app/Http/Controllers/Admin/StoreManagementController.php` (အသစ်) — `index / create / store / edit / update / destroy` — validation: `name` unique, `slug` lowercase unique regex, `is_active` boolean, `is_primary` ကို single-primary rule နဲ့ စစ်; deactivate ချိန် primary ဖြစ်နေရင် reassign လုပ်ပြီးမှ deactivate; product count (`withCount`) နဲ့ flash messages ပါ။
- **Middleware** `app/Http/Middleware/EnsurePlatformOwner.php` (အသစ်) + alias `platform.owner` — global admin group အတွင်း `/admin/stores*` routes ကို platform owner သာ ဝင်ခွင့် (store-scoped middleware မဟုတ် — ဘယ် store context မှာမဆို owner က store list မြင်ရမယ်)။
- **Routes** `routes/web.php` — `Route::middleware(['platform.owner'])->prefix('admin/stores')...` → `admin.stores.index/create/store/edit/update/destroy`.
- **Views** `resources/views/admin/stores/` — `index.blade.php` (table + primary badge + activate/deactivate modal, `x-data` scope အတွင်း modal — Alpine `:action` binding မှန်ကန်စွာ အလုပ်လုပ်ဖို့), `create.blade.php`, `edit.blade.php`, `_form.blade.php` (shared partial) — existing admin design system (brands/categories pattern) အတိုင်း။
- **Sidebar** `resources/views/layouts/admin/app.blade.php` — "စတိုး စီမံခန့်ခွဲမှု" link, platform owner မှသာ မြင်ရ (existing settings `@can` pattern အတိုင်း)။
- **Model** `app/Models/Store.php` — `products()` relation ထည့် (`withCount` အတွက်)။
- **Localization** `lang/{en,my,zh_CN}/messages.php` — store-management keys ၁၀ ခု (store_management, store_name, store_slug, stores, add_store, edit_store, deactivate_store, activate_store, store_deactivated, store_activated, primary_store စသည်) — သုံးဘာသာ တပြေးညီ။
- **Tests** `tests/Feature/AdminStoreManagementTest.php` — 12 tests / 42 assertions (plan §7.2): platform owner list 200 · non-owner 403 · create (valid + duplicate slug 422 + invalid slug) · edit · update · primary-single rule (primary ကို deactivate မရ) · deactivate + storefront 404 · reactivate + storefront 200 · last-active guard · delete hard-delete မလုပ် (soft state `is_active=false`) ။
- **Verified:** Full suite **626 passed / 2997 assertions** (614 + 12 အသစ်, regression 0). Live preview: login → sidebar link → list (primary badge + actions) → create ("Test Branch Store") → deactivate (modal → success flash "စတိုး ပိတ်လိုက်ပါပြီ။" + storefront 404) → reactivate (storefront 200) → test data cleanup — အကုန် PASS။ Preview မှာ CSP က Vite `localhost:5173` origin ကို block လုပ်လို့ Alpine/CSS မတင်တဲ့ issue တွေ့ခဲ့ — `public/hot` ဖျက်ပြီး built assets (same-origin) နဲ့ re-verify; modal scope bug တစ်ခုလည်း တွေ့ပြီး ပြင်ပြီး (index view မှာ modal ကို `x-data` div ထဲ ရွှေ့)။
- **Deploy:** `php artisan migrate` + `npm run build` (အသစ် Tailwind classes မရှိပေမယ့် built assets အတွက်) — route cache ပြန်ဆောက်ရန် `php artisan optimize:clear` + `php artisan route:cache`။
